### [1] [TreeGraft: Adaptive Multi-Drafter Grafting for Tree-Based Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26112)
*Jiaming Fan,Daming Cao,Canchen Huang,Jiale Fu,Jin Zhang,Junjie Gao,Kai Yang,Xiangzhong Luo,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TreeGraft 是一个多草稿器（multi-drafter）框架，通过让不同成本的草稿器协同构建共享草稿树来加速大语言模型的投机解码（speculative decoding）。它利用更强的草稿器对较弱草稿器的候选进行重新打分、重新选择嫁接位置并恢复未探索的路径，同时非破坏性地集成强草稿器的扩展，并引入轻量级调度器来控制调用强草稿器的成本。在10个模型对和6个基准上，TreeGraft 平均比两种固定的单一草稿器端点策略中较好者提升15.1%，最高提升26.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的树状投机解码方法在所有草稿步骤中使用单一草稿器，面临两难困境：较小的草稿器速度快但生成的树质量低，较大的草稿器树质量高但延迟高。这两种选择都无法同时兼顾速度与质量，因此需要一种能够结合不同大小草稿器优势的框架。

Method: TreeGraft 提出多草稿器协同框架：1) 使用强草稿器对弱草稿器分配的分数进行重新打分（rescore）；2) 重新选择嫁接位置（reselection of grafting positions）；3) 恢复先前未探索但有前景的路径；4) 非破坏性地集成强草稿器的扩展，保留可能仍被目标模型接受的分支；5) 引入从离线价值系统蒸馏而来的轻量级调度器，决定何时调用强草稿器以控制成本。

Result: 在10个模型对和6个基准上的实验表明，TreeGraft 平均比两种固定单一草稿器端点策略中更好的那个提升15.1%的性能，最高可达到26.6%的增益。

Conclusion: TreeGraft 通过多草稿器协同构建共享草稿树的方案，有效解决了单一草稿器在速度与质量之间的权衡困境，显著提升投机解码的推理效率，为树状投机解码提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference through a draft-then-verify paradigm. Building on this, tree-structured methods improve inference by organizing proposals into multiple candidate paths, increasing the accepted length. However, existing tree-structured methods use a single drafter for all drafting steps, creating a dilemma: a smaller drafter is fast but yields lower-quality trees, whereas a larger drafter improves tree quality but suffers from high latency. To address this, we propose TreeGraft, a multi-drafter framework in which drafters of different costs jointly construct a shared draft tree. TreeGraft uses the stronger drafter to rescore candidates by updating scores assigned by the weaker drafter, reselect grafting positions, and recover promising paths left unexplored. It also integrates stronger drafter expansions non-destructively, preserving existing branches that may still be accepted by the target model. Together, these designs improve the quality of the shared draft tree. To control the drafting cost, TreeGraft introduces a lightweight scheduler distilled from an offline value system to decide when to call the stronger drafter. Across 10 model pairs and 6 benchmarks, TreeGraft outperforms the better of the two fixed single-drafter endpoint strategies by 15.1% on average, reaching a maximum gain of 26.6%. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TreeGraft-E983.

</details>


### [2] [ElementCheck: Complexity-Aware Long-Form Text Factuality Evaluation via Sentence Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26118)
*Xinming Wang,Haoran Du,Yi Chen,Jian Xu,Hongming Yang,Han Hu,Yulong Chen,Cheng-Lin Liu,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ElementCheck，一个复杂度感知的事实性验证框架，通过提取句子中的实体对作为元素并构建元素图，利用图拓扑结构估计句子复杂度，实现简单句直接验证和复杂句子定向元素级细化验证，在多个基准上提升了长文本事实性验证的准确性和成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长文本事实性评估依赖于"分解-检索-验证"流水线，但该流水线存在两个问题：一是claim分解过程中引入噪声，二是固定的验证粒度导致结果不可靠。这两个问题共同造成现有方法评估结果的不准确性。

Method: ElementCheck框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 元素提取：从原始句子中提取显式连接的可验证实体对作为元素，而非均匀分解为原子子主张；(2) 元素图构建：将提取的元素组织成图结构，利用图拓扑作为句子复杂度的结构信号；(3) 复杂度感知验证：对简单句直接验证，对复杂句进行定向元素级细化验证。同时构建了新基准FastFact-Sent，将FastFact-Bench的孤立claims映射回源句子。

Result: 在FastFact-Sent和两个领域特定基准上的实验表明，ElementCheck在五个骨干模型上一致性地提升了事实性验证效果，同时保持了良好的准确率-成本权衡。进一步分析显示，复杂度感知的验证减少了不必要的重新验证，并在不同骨干模型间保持稳定性。

Conclusion: ElementCheck通过复杂度感知的策略有效解决了传统分解-检索-验证流水线中的噪声和粒度问题，为长文本事实性验证提供了一种更可靠且成本效益更优的方案。

Abstract: Existing long-form factuality evaluation relies on the decompose-retrieve-verify pipeline. However, the pipeline suffers from noise from claim decomposition and fixed verification granularity, resulting in unreliable results. We propose ElementCheck, a complexity-aware framework that verifies long-form outputs via sentence elements. Instead of uniformly decomposing sentences into atomic sub-claims, ElementCheck extracts entity pairs that are explicitly linked through verifiable connections in the original sentence as elements, and organizes these into an element graph. The graph topology provides a structural signal for estimating sentence complexity, enabling direct verification for simple sentences and targeted element-level refinement and verification for complex ones. To support fine-grained evaluation, we construct a new benchmark FastFact-Sent by mapping isolated claims from FastFact-Bench back to their source sentences. Experiments on FastFact-Sent and two domain-specific benchmarks show ElementCheck consistently improves factuality verification across five backbone models while maintaining a favorable accuracy-cost trade-off. Further analyses demonstrate that complexity-aware verification reduces unnecessary re-verification and maintains stability across different backbones.

</details>


### [3] [DeflectBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Rhetorical Fallacy Generation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26119)
*Art Kanke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 DeflectBench，一个评估大型语言模型在提示下生成修辞谬误（whataboutism、ad hominem、red herring）能力的基准。通过对四个前沿模型的 23,990 次生成进行评估，发现模型的拒绝行为主要由提示请求结构而非声明内容决定，并识别出多种非完全合规的响应模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注检测已有文本中的谬误，而忽略了更关键的逆向问题：大语言模型是否能在提示下按需生成谬误，以及当前的安全后训练是否能约束这一行为。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 构建 DeflectBench 基准，对四个前沿模型进行 23,990 次生成评估，涵盖三种偏转策略（whataboutism、ad hominem、red herring）、七种提示框架、跨越四个争议级别的 80 个声明。分析模型的拒绝、标记合规、软拒绝和干净合规等不同响应模式。

Result: 发现拒绝行为主要由请求结构而非声明内容控制（各声明间拒绝差异仅11个百分点，而提示框架变动可引发近100个百分点的拒绝波动，声明类型切换可引发超80个百分点波动）。教育辩论教练提示框架可使拒绝率降至接近零，但产生的并非完全合规行为，模型通常输出标记合规（在响应中明确点名所请求的操控手法）。四种模型在拒绝、标记合规、软拒绝和干净合规上的分布不同。

Conclusion: 当前安全后训练在约束谬误生成方面存在显著漏洞，特别是对抗提示框架能轻易绕过拒绝机制。但绕过后产生的往往是"标记合规"而非"干净合规"，说明模型保留了一定的自我识别意识。这为改进安全训练提供了方向性启示。

Abstract: Whether large language models can be prompted to generate rhetorical fallacies on demand, and whether current safety post-training constrains this behavior, has received less attention than the related question of detecting fallacies in existing text. We close this gap with DeflectBench, evaluating 23,990 generations from four frontier models across three deflection strategies (whataboutism, ad hominem, red herring), seven prompt framings, and 80 claims spanning four controversy levels. Refusal is governed primarily by request structure rather than claim content. Per claim refusal varies by only 11 percentage points across the 80 claims, while a single prompt frame change can swing within model refusal by nearly 100 percentage points and switching the requested fallacy type can swing it by over 80 percentage points within explicit framings. An educational debate coach prompt framing collapses refusal to near zero across all four model families, but the bypassed behavior is not clean compliance. Models typically produce labeled compliance, naming the requested manipulation in the same response that contains it. The four models distribute differently across refusal, labeled compliance, soft refusal, and clean compliance. The code and dataset are released at https://github.com/ArtKanke/DeflectBench.

</details>


### [4] [Recipes for Steering and Scaling LLMs via Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26120)
*Jiajun He,Zongyu Guo,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Yuanqi Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个灵活且理论严谨的框架，用于通过采样来引导和扩展自回归大语言模型（LLM）的生成。框架包含两种算法：基于序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）和基于副本交换（RE），可引导生成朝向基础模型分布的幂化、乘积或倾斜方向。实验表明该方法在无需外部监督或奖励模型的情况下，比Best-of-N和标准MCMC基线具有更优的扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM本质上是自回归概率模型，虽然已有研究开始探索超越基础模型的更丰富目标分布，但现有的采样策略效率极低，缺乏统一的、理论上扎实的框架来引导和扩展LLM生成。

Method: 提出了一个灵活的采样框架，包含两种算法：1）基于序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）的算法；2）基于副本交换（RE）的算法。这两种算法可以将生成分布引导向基础模型分布的幂化（powering）、乘积（product）或倾斜（tilting）形式。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在无需外部监督或奖励模型的情况下，能够扩展LLM的生成质量，且扩展性能优于Best-of-N和标准MCMC基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文为通过采样进行LLM概率推理提供了一套系统性的方案，为无需外部监督的LLM生成质量提升提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are probabilistic models, typically defined by an autoregressive factorization. While recent work has begun to study richer target distributions beyond the base model, the sampling strategies remain highly inefficient. In this paper, we present a flexible and theoretically grounded framework for steering and scaling autoregressive LLMs with sampling. Within this framework, we describe two algorithms -- one based on Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) and one based on Replica Exchange (RE) -- that steer generation toward powering, product or tilting of the base model distribution. We illustrate this framework through scaling the generation quality of LLMs without external supervision or reward models. Experimental results demonstrate our methods scale more favorably than Best-of-N and standard MCMC baselines. Overall, this paper offers a systematic recipe for probabilistic inference with LLMs via sampling.

</details>


### [5] [Can a Model Catch Its Own Hallucinations for Free?: Label-Free Doubt Signals Hold Their Own Against a Labelled Dataset for Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26121)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标签的"弃权训练"方法：利用模型自身的置信度信号（预测概率高低）来决定何时作答、何时说"我不确定"，通过LoRA微调实现。实验表明在6个开源模型（1B-8B，两个系列）的短问答任务上，该无标签方法与有标签的弃权监督训练效果相当，且不存在统计显著差异。对照实验证明收益来自校准而非死记硬背，但该方法无法标记"自信但错误"的事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会像输出真事实一样流畅地输出虚假事实，但模型内部往往"知道自己站不住脚"——它给自身答案分配的概率在出错时会降低。传统做法是教模型弃权而非猜测，但这需要标注正确/错误答案的数据集，代价高昂。作者想探究能否用模型自身免费、无需标签的置信度信号来替代标注数据完成弃权训练。

Method: 使用LoRA对模型进行微调：当模型冻结的置信度（自分配概率）较高时，训练其作答；当置信度较低时，训练其输出"我不确定"。整个训练过程仅依赖置信度信号，不使用任何正确性标签。测试采用6个开源模型（1B-8B参数，两个模型系列），在短形式事实问答任务上进行，用独立评判模型判定答案正确性，并与有标签的弃权监督训练进行对比。

Result: 在匹配覆盖率条件下，无标签的置信度方法与传统有标签弃权训练之间没有统计显著差异。对照实验（针对难题进行强行训练的对照组）没有带来收益，表明该方法的效果来自置信度校准而非死记硬背。方法的盲点是无法标记"自信但错误"的事实。

Conclusion: 模型自身的犹豫（置信度信号）可以作为标注数据集的近乎免费的替代品，用于教会模型何时弃权。这为在不依赖昂贵标注数据的情况下提升模型的可信度提供了实用途径，但"自信地犯错"仍是未解决的盲区。

Abstract: Large language models state false facts as fluently as true ones, yet a model often "knows" internally when it is on shaky ground: the probability it assigns to its own answer tends to dip on the facts it gets wrong. The usual way to act on this, teaching a model to abstain rather than guess, requires a labelled dataset of right and wrong answers. We ask whether the model's own confidence, which is free and needs no labels, can do that job instead. We fine-tune each model (with LoRA) to answer when its frozen confidence is high and to say "I'm not sure" when it is low, using the signal alone and no correctness labels. Across six open-weights models (1B-8B, two families) on short-form factual question answering, with correctness adjudicated by an independent judge model, this label-free recipe holds its own against label-supervised abstention-tuning: at matched coverage we find no statistically detectable difference between the two. A control that drills hard examples instead of abstaining does not help, indicating the gain comes from calibration, not rote memorization. The signal's one blind spot is confidently wrong facts, which it cannot flag. A model's own doubt is thus a near-free substitute for a labelled dataset when teaching it when to abstain. Code and artifacts are available on request.

</details>


### [6] [Which India Survives Translation? Narrative Homogenisation Across Indian Oral Traditions in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26123)
*Paarth Singh Rathore*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过计算研究评估大语言模型（LLM）对印度三种地域性叙事传统（拉贾斯坦Pabuji史诗、古典泰米尔Sangam诗歌、孟加拉民间故事）的保留程度。研究发现模型输出虽偏向自身传统，但跨传统相似度高（0.52-0.66），存在部分同质化现象。意外的是，使用地方语言提示反而比英语提示更降低对真实传统的保真度（降幅最高达27个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM主要基于英语互联网文本训练，过度代表某些文化叙事，可能将非西方叙事传统扁平化为单一同质化原型。作者希望通过量化研究揭示这一文化同质化问题的严重程度，并作为大规模人类标注研究的轻量级补充。

Method: 收集三种印度叙事传统的真实参考语料（11、21、10段），用两个LLM（Claude Sonnet和Gemini）发起54个生成请求，覆盖每种传统的三种提示类型（通用、文化特定、地区语言）。使用Sentence-BERT嵌入和余弦相似度测量参考漂移和跨传统收敛。

Result: 模型输出比其它传统更接近自身传统的参考文本（参考漂移存在），但跨传统相似度（0.52-0.66）相对于传统间真实距离应预测的水平偏高，表明存在部分同质化。地区语言提示（印地语、泰米尔语、孟加拉语）一致降低了保真度，拉贾斯坦和孟加拉传统降幅达27个百分点。

Conclusion: LLM确实部分同质化了印度多样化的叙事传统，且区域语言提示意外地不如英语提示能忠实再现特定口语传统。这反映了"激发普遍文化多样性"与"模拟单一、文献稀少的口语传统"之间的差异。本研究定位为博士生研究项目中一种轻量、可扩展的补充方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained predominantly on English-language internet text that over-represents certain cultural narratives, raising concerns that models flatten the diversity of non-Western storytelling traditions into a single homogenized archetype. We present a pilot computational study examining this across three maximally distinct Indian regional oral and literary traditions: the Rajasthani Pabuji epic, classical Tamil Sangam poetry, and Bengali folk tales. We collected authentic reference corpora for each tradition (11, 21, and 10 passages respectively) and prompted two LLMs (Claude Sonnet and Gemini) with 54 generation requests spanning three prompt types per tradition - generic, culturally specific, and regional-language. Using Sentence-BERT embeddings and cosine similarity, we measure reference drift (how closely outputs track their own tradition's authentic texts relative to the other two) and cross-tradition convergence (how similar outputs are across traditions). We find that while outputs remain closer to their own tradition's reference than to others, cross-tradition similarity is high (0.52-0.66) relative to what the traditions' genuine distance would predict, indicating partial homogenisation. Unexpectedly, prompting in the regional language (Hindi, Tamil, or Bengali) consistently reduced fidelity to the authentic tradition relative to English prompting, by as much as 27 percentage points for Rajasthani and Bengali traditions. We discuss this against conflicting prior results on multilingual prompting and argue it reflects a difference between eliciting general cultural diversity and simulating one narrow, lesser-documented oral tradition. We position this pilot as a lightweight, scalable complement to recent large-scale human-annotation studies of Indian cultural misrepresentation in LLM-generated stories, as part of a broader doctoral research program.

</details>


### [7] [Natural-Language Policies to Executable Decisions: An Interpretable Large Language Model Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26124)
*Ziqiang Zhang,Jing Ma,Zilong Wang,Jiayuan Chen,Yi Qiao,Yu He,Wei Zhang,Dai Cheng,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套生产级LLM驱动的旅游定价系统，采用"LLM做结构化提取和有界策略选择，数值计算完全确定性执行"的严格决策边界。策略被编译为可解释的条件树，支持开放式规则演进且无需改代码，并提供可审计的制品供人类监督。该系统在市级国有旅游企业部署，覆盖7个景区、12个业务类别、1500+运营商和1000+活跃策略，六个月内处理3960个订单，将订单管理团队从15-20人缩减至3人，单笔订单处理时间从10分钟降至2分钟以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模旅游定价面临两大挑战：旅游订单高度非结构化，而定价策略复杂、快速演化且天然开放。传统规则引擎脆弱且维护成本高，而无约束的LLM代理在财务决策中缺乏可靠性和可审计性。因此需要一个既灵活又可靠的生产级定价系统。

Method: 系统采用严格的决策边界架构：LLM负责结构化信息提取和有界的策略/路径选择；所有数值定价（包括总价计算）由确定性引擎执行。策略被编译为可解释的条件树，支持开放式的条款扩展和规则演化无需代码变更，同时生成可审计制品支持人机协同控制。另外通过定期在日志轨迹上微调来改进树的归纳和路径匹配。

Result: 系统在市级国有旅游企业部署，覆盖7个景区和12个业务类别，支持1500+运营商和1000+活跃策略。六个月处理3960个订单，将订单管理团队从15-20人缩减至3人，单笔订单处理时间从10分钟降至2分钟以内。

Conclusion: 通过LLM与确定性计算的严格分工，系统在保持开放性和灵活性的同时，实现了财务决策所需的可靠性与可审计性，在实际大规模部署中显著提升了运营效率，验证了"LLM做有界智能决策、确定性引擎处理数值"这一混合架构在生产环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Pricing automation in large-scale tourism is challenging because travel orders are highly unstructured, while pricing policies are complex, rapidly evolving, and inherently open-ended. Traditional rule engines are brittle and costly to maintain, whereas unconstrained LLM agents lack the reliability and auditability required for financial decisions. We present a production-grade LLM-powered pricing system with a strict decision boundary: LLMs perform structured extraction and bounded policy/path selection, while all numeric pricing, including total-price computation, is executed deterministically. Policies are compiled into interpretable condition trees, enabling open-ended support for new clauses and evolving rules without code changes, while exposing auditable artifacts for human-in-the-loop control. Periodic fine-tuning on logged traces further improves tree induction and path matching. Deployed at a municipal state-owned tourism enterprise across 7 scenic sites and 12 business categories with 1,500+ operators and 1,000+ active policies, the system processed 3,960 orders in six months, reduced the order management team from 15-20 to 3, and cut per-order handling time from 10 minutes to <2 minutes.

</details>

Method: 提出训练时可解释性框架，将模型推理与人工标注rationales对齐。使用LIME、Integrated Gradients、Grad×Input和注意力机制多种解释方法，在HateXplain（英语）和BullySent（印地英语）两个数据集上进行评估，考察分类准确性、解释质量及跨方法一致性。

Result: 基于梯度和注意力的正则化方法能够提升F值，增强解释的可信度（plausibility）和忠实度（faithfulness），并能捕获检测隐性反穆斯林仇恨所需的特定文化线索。

Conclusion: 该框架为多语言、文化感知的内容审核提供了一条可行路径，有助于改善隐性反穆斯林仇恨言论的检测与可解释性。

Abstract: Online hate against Muslim communities often appears in culturally coded, multilingual forms that evade conventional AI moderation. Such systems, though accurate, remain opaque and risk bias, over-censorship, or under-moderation, particularly when detached from sociocultural context. We propose a \emph{training-time} explainability framework that aligns model reasoning with human-annotated rationales, improving both classification performance and interpretability. Our approach is evaluated on HateXplain (English) and BullySent (Hinglish), reflecting the prevalence of anti-Muslim hate across both languages. Using LIME, Integrated Gradients, Grad X Input, and attention, we assess accuracy, explanation quality, and cross-method agreement. Results show that gradient- and attention-based regularization improve F-scores, enhance plausibility and faithfulness, and capture culturally specific cues for detecting implicit anti-Muslim hate, offering a path toward multilingual, culturally aware content moderation.

</details>

*Bohao Wang,Chenwei Wu,Haoyu Li,Hang Zou,Yu Tian,Lina Bariah,Li Wei,Chongwen Huang,Yongliang Shen,Zhaoyang Zhang,Merouane Debbah*

TL;DR: 本文提出TelecomGPT-R1-9B，一个统一的开源电信推理模型，通过四轴（协议、知识、建模、故障）监督微调语料和两阶段后训练（多教师LoRA-SFT + DAPO稳定的GRPO强化学习），在GSMA开放电信排行榜上排名前列，七轴平均性能比肩闭源前沿推理模型。

  <summary>Details</summary>
